{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1790681734870,"sessionID":"ses_f130e8eecffevMANL5K76zMuzF","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_00_vcvI2WnGhUry4iICHc6l8400","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_validate.py"},"output":"<path>C:\\Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_validate.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"Validate agent definitions (AGENT.md).\n2: \n3: Part of the agent-creator skill (see SKILL.md).\n4: Checks frontmatter schema, identity/boundary/permission/collaboration\n5: sections, and dangling local references.\n6: \n7: Usage:\n8:     python scripts/agent_validate.py [--dir <agents_dir>] [--strict]\n9: \n10: Exit code 0 = all passed, 1 = errors found (or warnings in strict mode).\n11: \"\"\"\n12: \n13: import argparse\n14: import io\n15: import os\n16: import re\n17: import sys\n18: from collections.abc import Mapping\n19: from datetime import date, datetime\n20: \n21: import yaml\n22: \n23: from _project_paths import find_skill_root\n24: from agent_security import (\n25:     fenced_ranges,\n26:     find_dangerous_pipes,\n27:     find_inline_secrets,\n28:     is_scannable_text,\n29: )\n30: \n31: # Skill root = the directory containing scripts/ (self-contained; the module\n32: # never depends on a host repository). Used as the fallback base for backtick\n33: # references and as the default scan target.\n34: SKILL_ROOT = find_skill_root(__file__)\n35: # Doc/resource dirs that must never be scanned as agent definitions\n36: EXEMPT_DIRS = {\"examples\", \"references\", \"templates\"}\n37: \n38: # Noise dirs skipped by the security sweep. Unlike definition discovery, the\n39: # sweep must descend into *hidden* dirs: credentials commonly live in `.ssh/`,\n40: # `.aws/`, etc., so skipping every dotdir let a bundled `id_rsa` slip through.\n41: # Only VCS/cache trees that cannot hold authored text are skipped.\n42: SECURITY_SKIP_DIRS = {\n43:     \".git\", \".hg\", \".svn\", \"__pycache__\", \"node_modules\",\n44:     \".mypy_cache\", \".pytest_cache\", \".ruff_cache\", \".tox\", \".venv\", \"venv\",\n45: }\n46: \n47: # Non-agent docs that may sit beside agents in a library (also used by\n48: # agent_compare.py). Kept lowercase for case-insensitive matching.\n49: NON_AGENT_DOCS = {\n50:     \"readme.md\", \"readme\", \"changelog.md\", \"development-plan.md\",\n51:     \"agents.md\", \"skill.md\", \"catalog.md\", \"catalog\", \"agents-audit.md\",\n52: }\n53: \n54: # Backtick resource references that must resolve on disk. The extension\n55: # whitelist is broader than script types so data/text artifacts\n56: # (`indexes/upstream.db`, `.txt`, …) are covered too. Refs inside fenced code\n57: # blocks are illustrative and exempt.\n58: BACKTICK_REF_RE = re.compile(\n59:     r\"`([^`\\s]+\\.(?:md|py|sh|json|ya?ml|toml|txt|xml|db|sqlite|csv|ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs|css|html|rs|go|java|rb|php))`\"\n60: )\n61: \n62: VALID_NAME = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\")\n63: \n64: # Optional UI display color. Matches agent_adapt.py's opencode post-check:\n65: # a #RRGGBB hex value or one of the opencode theme names. In YAML a `#` starts\n66: # a comment, so a hex color MUST be quoted (`color: \"#DC2626\"`); an unquoted\n67: # value parses as None and is rejected here (which also catches copy-paste slips).\n68: VALID_COLOR = re.compile(r\"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$\")\n69: COLOR_THEMES = (\"primary\", \"secondary\", \"accent\", \"success\", \"warning\", \"error\", \"info\")\n70: \n71: BOUNDARY_PATTERNS = [\n72:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+职责范围\", re.MULTILINE),\n73:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Responsibilities?\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n74:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Scope\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n75: ]\n76: MUST_DO_PATTERNS = [\n77:     re.compile(r\"必须做\", re.MULTILINE),\n78:     re.compile(r\"Must\\s+Do\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n79: ]\n80: REFUSE_PATTERNS = [\n81:     re.compile(r\"拒绝做\", re.MULTILINE),\n82:     re.compile(r\"Refuse|Decline|Never\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n83: ]\n84: PERMISSION_PATTERNS = [\n85:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+工具与权限\", re.MULTILINE),\n86:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Tools?\\s*(?:&|and)\\s*Permissions?\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n87: ]\n88: COLLAB_PATTERNS = [\n89:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+协作协议\", re.MULTILINE),\n90:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Collaboration\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n91: ]\n92: ESCALATION_PATTERNS = [\n93:     re.compile(r\"升级路径|升级|交还\", re.MULTILINE),\n94:     re.compile(r\"Escalat\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n95: ]\n96: COMPLETION_PATTERNS = [\n97:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+完成标准\", re.MULTILINE),\n98:     re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Completion\\s*(?:Criteria|Standard)\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n99: ]\n100: TOOLS_FIELD_PATTERNS = [\n101:     re.compile(r\"^tools:\", re.MULTILINE),\n102:     re.compile(r\"^permission:\", re.MULTILINE),\n103: ]\n104: SECURITY_DISCLAIMER_PATTERNS = [\n105:     re.compile(r\"AUTHORIZED USE ONLY\", re.IGNORECASE),\n106:     re.compile(r\"仅限授权使用\"),\n107: ]\n108: \n109: \n110: def configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n111:     if sys.platform != \"win32\":\n112:         return\n113:     for stream_name in (\"stdout\", \"stderr\"):\n114:         stream = getattr(sys, stream_name)\n115:         try:\n116:             stream.reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\")\n117:             continue\n118:         except Exception:\n119:             pass\n120:         buffer = getattr(stream, \"buffer\", None)\n121:         if buffer is not None:\n122:             setattr(\n123:                 sys,\n124:                 stream_name,\n125:                 io.TextIOWrapper(buffer, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\"),\n126:             )\n127: \n128: \n129: def is_exempt_dir(path: str) -> bool:\n130:     parts = path.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").split(\"/\")\n131:     return any(p in EXEMPT_DIRS for p in parts)\n132: \n133: \n134: def normalize_yaml_value(value):\n135:     if isinstance(value, Mapping):\n136:         return {k: normalize_yaml_value(v) for k, v in value.items()}\n137:     if isinstance(value, list):\n138:         return [normalize_yaml_value(v) for v in value]\n139:     if isinstance(value, (date, datetime)):\n140:         return value.isoformat()\n141:     return value\n142: \n143: \n144: def parse_frontmatter(content: str):\n145:     fm_match = re.search(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)\", content, re.DOTALL)\n146:     if not fm_match:\n147:         return None, [\"Missing or malformed YAML frontmatter\"]\n148:     fm_text = fm_match.group(1)\n149:     fm_errors = []\n150:     try:\n151:         metadata = yaml.safe_load(fm_text) or {}\n152:         metadata = normalize_yaml_value(metadata)\n153:         if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):\n154:             return None, [\"Frontmatter must be a YAML mapping/object.\"]\n155:         if \"description\" in metadata:\n156:             desc = metadata[\"description\"]\n157:             if not desc or (isinstance(desc, str) and not desc.strip()):\n158:                 fm_errors.append(\"description field is empty or whitespace only.\")\n159:         return dict(metadata), fm_errors\n160:     except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n161:         return None, [f\"YAML Syntax Error: {e}\"]\n162: \n163: \n164: def agent_name_from_path(agent_path: str) -> str:\n165:     \"\"\"Determine the agent name from its file/dir name (strip trailing .md).\"\"\"\n166:     name = os.path.basename(agent_path)\n167:     if name.endswith(\".md\"):\n168:         name = name[:-3]\n169:     return name\n170: \n171: \n172: def check_dir_security(agent_dir: str, agent_file: str, rel_path: str) -> list[str]:\n173:     \"\"\"Scan every text/code file bundled with an agent for secrets / dangerous pipes.\n174: \n175:     Release discipline says to sweep the agent directory and its scripts before\n176:     publishing; a credential or `curl … | bash` pasted into a bundled resource\n177:     is as harmful as one in AGENT.md. Markdown files use the fenced-only\n178:     (prose-aware) view; code files are scanned in full.\n179:     \"\"\"\n180:     errs: list[str] = []\n181:     if not os.path.isdir(agent_dir):\n182:         return errs\n183:     agent_file_abs = os.path.abspath(agent_file)\n184:     for dirpath, dirs, files in os.walk(agent_dir):\n185:         # Exempt dirs (references/templates/examples) are excluded from agent-\n186:         # *definition* discovery, but bundled resources are exactly what the\n187:         # security sweep must cover — descend into hidden dirs (credential\n188:         # locations such as `.ssh/`/`.aws/`), skipping only VCS/cache trees.\n189:         dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SECURITY_SKIP_DIRS]\n190:         for fn in files:\n191:             p = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)\n192:             if os.path.abspath(p) == agent_file_abs or not is_scannable_text(fn):\n193:                 continue\n194:             try:\n195:                 with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n196:                     text = f.read()\n197:             except OSError:\n198:                 continue\n199:             is_markdown = fn.lower().endswith(\".md\")\n200:             for m in find_dangerous_pipes(text, fenced_only=is_markdown):\n201:                 errs.append(\n202:                     f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Dangerous remote-execution pipe detected in \"\n203:                     f\"{os.path.relpath(p, agent_dir)} ({m.group(0)[:60]!r}); remove it or annotate \"\n204:                     \"its block/line with a `<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n205:                 )\n206:             for m in find_inline_secrets(text):\n207:                 errs.append(\n208:                     f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Possible inline secret/credential detected in \"\n209:                     f\"{os.path.relpath(p, agent_dir)} ({m.group(0)[:6]}…); remove it or annotate \"\n210:                     \"its line with a `<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n211:                 )\n212:     return errs\n213: \n214: \n215: def collect_validation_results(agents_dir: str, strict_mode: bool = False) -> dict:\n216:     agents_dir = os.path.abspath(agents_dir)\n217:     errors = []\n218:     if not os.path.isdir(agents_dir):\n219:         # A wrong/typo'd --dir must FAIL loudly. os.walk on a missing path yields\n220:         # nothing, so without this guard we'd print \"Checked 0 agents\" and exit 0.\n221:         errors.append(f\"❌ Scan directory does not exist: {agents_dir}\")\n222:         return {\n223:             \"agent_count\": 0,\n224:             \"warnings\": [],\n225:             \"advisories\": [],\n226:             \"errors\": errors,\n227:             \"strict_mode\": strict_mode,\n228:         }\n229:     warnings = []\n230:     advisories = []\n231:     agent_count = 0\n232: \n233:     for root, dirs, files in os.walk(agents_dir):\n234:         dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if not d.startswith(\".\") and not is_exempt_dir(os.path.join(root, d))]\n235:         # Agent definition files: AGENT.md inside a dir, or a top-level *.md\n236:         if \"AGENT.md\" in files:\n237:             candidates = [(root, \"AGENT.md\")]\n238:         else:\n239:             mds = sorted(f for f in files if f.endswith(\".md\") and f.lower() not in NON_AGENT_DOCS)\n240:             candidates = [(root, f) for f in mds]\n241:         for base, fname in candidates:\n242:             agent_path = os.path.join(base, fname)\n243:             rel_path = os.path.relpath(agent_path, agents_dir)\n244:             try:\n245:                 with open(agent_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n246:                     content = f.read()\n247:             except Exception as e:\n248:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Unreadable file - {str(e)}\")\n249:                 continue\n250: \n251:             # Only AGENT.md is a mandatory marker. A plain *.md is an agent\n252:             # definition only when it actually carries frontmatter, so library\n253:             # docs (evolution records, subagent prompt files) are not mistaken\n254:             # for agents and blamed for \"missing frontmatter\".\n255:             if fname != \"AGENT.md\" and not re.match(r\"^---\\s*\\n\", content):\n256:                 continue\n257: \n258:             agent_count += 1\n259:             metadata, fm_errors = parse_frontmatter(content)\n260:             if metadata is None:\n261:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing or malformed YAML frontmatter\")\n262:                 continue\n263:             for fe in fm_errors:\n264:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: YAML Structure Error - {fe}\")\n265: \n266:             dir_name = agent_name_from_path(root) if fname == \"AGENT.md\" else agent_name_from_path(fname)\n267:             if \"name\" not in metadata:\n268:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing 'name' in frontmatter\")\n269:             else:\n270:                 n = metadata[\"name\"]\n271:                 if not isinstance(n, str):\n272:                     errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'name' must be a string, got {type(n).__name__}\")\n273:                 else:\n274:                     if not VALID_NAME.match(n):\n275:                         errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'name' must be kebab-case, got '{n}'\")\n276:                     if n != dir_name:\n277:                         warnings.append(f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: name '{n}' differs from dir/file name '{dir_name}'\")\n278: \n279:             if \"description\" not in metadata or metadata[\"description\"] is None:\n280:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing 'description' in frontmatter\")\n281:             else:\n282:                 desc = metadata[\"description\"]\n283:                 if not isinstance(desc, str):\n284:                     errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'description' must be a string, got {type(desc).__name__}\")\n285:                 elif len(desc) > 300:\n286:                     errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Description is oversized ({len(desc)} chars). Unknown leftover\n319:             # fields are simply ignored (forward-compatible).\n320: \n321:             body = content.split(\"---\", 2)[2] if content.startswith(\"---\") else content\n322:             if not body.strip():\n323:                 errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Agent body is empty (identity/boundary sections required)\")\n324: \n325:             if not any(p.search(content) for p in BOUNDARY_PATTERNS):\n326:                 msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: Missing '## 职责范围' section (identity boundary required)\"\n327:                 (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n328:             elif not (any(p.search(content) for p in MUST_DO_PATTERNS) and any(p.search(content) for p in REFUSE_PATTERNS)):\n329:                 msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: '职责范围' should declare both 必须做 and 拒绝做\"\n330:                 (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n331: \n332:             if not any(p.search(content) for p in PERMISSION_PATTERNS) and not any(\n333:                 p.search(content) for p in TOOLS_FIELD_PATTERNS\n334:             ):\n335:                 msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: No tools/permission declaration (least-privilege principle)\"\n336:                 (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n337: \n338:             perm_map = metadata.get(\"permission\")\n339:             if isinstance(perm_map, dict) and \"*\" not in perm_map:\n340:                 # CommonMark allows an optional title after the destination\n371:                 # (`[x](path \"Title\")`). Strip it so a valid titled link is not\n372:                 # mistaken for a path containing spaces and reported dangling.\n373:                 link = re.sub(r\"\\s+(?:\\\"[^\\\"]*\\\"|'[^']*'|\\([^)]*\\))\\s*$\", \"\", link).strip()\n374:                 link_clean = link.split(\"#\")[0].strip()\n375:                 if not link_clean or link_clean.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\", \"mailto:\", \"<\", \">\")):\n376:                     continue\n377:                 if os.path.isabs(link_clean):\n378:                     continue\n379:                 target_path = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root, link_clean))\n380:                 if not os.path.exists(target_path):\n381:                     errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Dangling link detected. Falling back\n402:                 # to this validator's own SKILL_ROOT would let an agent \"borrow\" a\n403:                 # path that only exists inside agent-creator — a false pass that\n404:                 # hides dangling references.\n405:                 target = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root, ref_clean))\n406:                 if not os.path.exists(target):\n407:                     errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Backtick reference '{ref_clean}' does not exist locally.\")\n408: \n409:             # Security scan: inline secrets everywhere; dangerous remote-exec\n410:             # pipes in code context. Local `<!-- security-allowlist -->` markers\n411:             # excuse only the annotated line/block.\n412:             for m in find_dangerous_pipes(content):\n413:                 errors.append(\n414:                     f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Dangerous remote-execution pipe detected \"\n415:                     f\"({m.group(0)[:60]!r}); remove it or annotate its block/line with a \"\n416:                     \"`<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n417:                 )\n418:             for m in find_inline_secrets(content):\n419:                 errors.append(\n420:                     f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Possible inline secret/credential detected \"\n421:                     f\"({m.group(0)[:6]}…); remove it or annotate its line with a \"\n422:                     \"`<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n423:                 )\n424:             if fname == \"AGENT.md\":\n425:                 errors.extend(check_dir_security(base, agent_path, rel_path))\n426: \n427:     if agent_count == 0:\n428:         # Scanning zero agent definitions is almost always a wrong target (typo'd\n429:         # --dir, or run from a directory that holds no AGENT.md). Fail loudly\n430:         # instead of a green, empty release gate. Pointing it there is a\n436:         # usage error, so still fail, but say so explicitly instead of letting the\n437:         # user suspect the source tree is incomplete.\n438:         hint = \"\"\n439:         if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(agents_dir, \"SKILL.md\")):\n440:             hint = (\n441:                 \" The target is a skill-form product root (SKILL.md present, no \"\n442:                 \"AGENT.md), not an agent library: agent-creator is a skill installed \"\n443:                 \"into the client's skills/ dir, and agent_validate.py only validates \"\n444:                 \"agent definitions. To self-check an installed agent-creator, run the \"\n445:                 \"roundtrip from SKILL.md 自安装 step 4 (agent_create.py → \"\n446:                 \"agent_validate.py --dir <生成的 install-check 目录>), or point --dir \"\n447:                 \"at an agents/ library.\"\n448:             )\n449:         errors.append(\n450:             f\"❌ No agent definitions found under: {agents_dir} \"\n451:             \"(wrong --dir? point it at a directory containing AGENT.md; the default \"\n452:             \"target is the current working directory)\"\n453:             f\"{hint}\"\n454: agent definitions (AGENT.md).\n\nPart of the agent-creator skill (see SKILL.md).\nChecks frontmatter schema, identity/boundary/permission/collaboration\nsections, and dangling local references.\n\nUsage:\n    python scripts/agent_validate.py [--dir <agents_dir>] [--strict]\n\nExit code 0 = all passed, 1 = errors found (or warnings in strict mode).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport io\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\nfrom datetime import date, datetime\n\nimport yaml\n\nfrom _project_paths import find_skill_root\nfrom agent_security import (\n    fenced_ranges,\n    find_dangerous_pipes,\n    find_inline_secrets,\n    is_scannable_text,\n)\n\n# Skill root = the directory containing scripts/ (self-contained; the module\n# never depends on a host repository). Used as the fallback base for backtick\n# references and as the default scan target.\nSKILL_ROOT = find_skill_root(__file__)\n# Doc/resource dirs that must never be scanned as agent definitions\nEXEMPT_DIRS = {\"examples\", \"references\", \"templates\"}\n\n# Noise dirs skipped by the security sweep. Unlike definition discovery, the\n# sweep must descend into *hidden* dirs: credentials commonly live in `.ssh/`,\n# `.aws/`, etc., so skipping every dotdir let a bundled `id_rsa` slip through.\n# Only VCS/cache trees that cannot hold authored text are skipped.\nSECURITY_SKIP_DIRS = {\n    \".git\", \".hg\", \".svn\", \"__pycache__\", \"node_modules\",\n    \".mypy_cache\", \".pytest_cache\", \".ruff_cache\", \".tox\", \".venv\", \"venv\",\n}\n\n# Non-agent docs that may sit beside agents in a library (also used by\n# agent_compare.py). Kept lowercase for case-insensitive matching.\nNON_AGENT_DOCS = {\n    \"readme.md\", \"readme\", \"changelog.md\", \"development-plan.md\",\n    \"agents.md\", \"skill.md\", \"catalog.md\", \"catalog\", \"agents-audit.md\",\n}\n\n# Backtick resource references that must resolve on disk. The extension\n# whitelist is broader than script types so data/text artifacts\n# (`indexes/upstream.db`, `.txt`, …) are covered too. Refs inside fenced code\n# blocks are illustrative and exempt.\nBACKTICK_REF_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"`([^`\\s]+\\.(?:md|py|sh|json|ya?ml|toml|txt|xml|db|sqlite|csv|ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs|css|html|rs|go|java|rb|php))`\"\n)\n\nVALID_NAME = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\")\n\n# Optional UI display color. Matches agent_adapt.py's opencode post-check:\n# a #RRGGBB hex value or one of the opencode theme names. In YAML a `#` starts\n# a comment, so a hex color MUST be quoted (`color: \"#DC2626\"`); an unquoted\n# value parses as None and is rejected here (which also catches copy-paste slips).\nVALID_COLOR = re.compile(r\"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$\")\nCOLOR_THEMES = (\"primary\", \"secondary\", \"accent\", \"success\", \"warning\", \"error\", \"info\")\n\nBOUNDARY_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+职责范围\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Responsibilities?\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Scope\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nMUST_DO_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"必须做\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"Must\\s+Do\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nREFUSE_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"拒绝做\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"Refuse|Decline|Never\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nPERMISSION_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+工具与权限\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Tools?\\s*(?:&|and)\\s*Permissions?\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nCOLLAB_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+协作协议\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Collaboration\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nESCALATION_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"升级路径|升级|交还\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"Escalat\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nCOMPLETION_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+完成标准\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"^##\\s+Completion\\s*(?:Criteria|Standard)\", re.MULTILINE | re.IGNORECASE),\n]\nTOOLS_FIELD_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"^tools:\", re.MULTILINE),\n    re.compile(r\"^permission:\", re.MULTILINE),\n]\nSECURITY_DISCLAIMER_PATTERNS = [\n    re.compile(r\"AUTHORIZED USE ONLY\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"仅限授权使用\"),\n]\n\n\ndef configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n    if sys.platform != \"win32\":\n        return\n    for stream_name in (\"stdout\", \"stderr\"):\n        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name)\n        try:\n            stream.reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\")\n            continue\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        buffer = getattr(stream, \"buffer\", None)\n        if buffer is not None:\n            setattr(\n                sys,\n                stream_name,\n                io.TextIOWrapper(buffer, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\"),\n            )\n\n\ndef is_exempt_dir(path: str) -> bool:\n    parts = path.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\").split(\"/\")\n    return any(p in EXEMPT_DIRS for p in parts)\n\n\ndef normalize_yaml_value(value):\n    if isinstance(value, Mapping):\n        return {k: normalize_yaml_value(v) for k, v in value.items()}\n    if isinstance(value, list):\n        return [normalize_yaml_value(v) for v in value]\n    if isinstance(value, (date, datetime)):\n        return value.isoformat()\n    return value\n\n\ndef parse_frontmatter(content: str):\n    fm_match = re.search(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)\", content, re.DOTALL)\n    if not fm_match:\n        return None, [\"Missing or malformed YAML frontmatter\"]\n    fm_text = fm_match.group(1)\n    fm_errors = []\n    try:\n        metadata = yaml.safe_load(fm_text) or {}\n        metadata = normalize_yaml_value(metadata)\n        if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):\n            return None, [\"Frontmatter must be a YAML mapping/object.\"]\n        if \"description\" in metadata:\n            desc = metadata[\"description\"]\n            if not desc or (isinstance(desc, str) and not desc.strip()):\n                fm_errors.append(\"description field is empty or whitespace only.\")\n        return dict(metadata), fm_errors\n    except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n        return None, [f\"YAML Syntax Error: {e}\"]\n\n\ndef agent_name_from_path(agent_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Determine the agent name from its file/dir name (strip trailing .md).\"\"\"\n    name = os.path.basename(agent_path)\n    if name.endswith(\".md\"):\n        name = name[:-3]\n    return name\n\n\ndef check_dir_security(agent_dir: str, agent_file: str, rel_path: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Scan every text/code file bundled with an agent for secrets / dangerous pipes.\n\n    Release discipline says to sweep the agent directory and its scripts before\n    publishing; a credential or `curl … | bash` pasted into a bundled resource\n    is as harmful as one in AGENT.md. Markdown files use the fenced-only\n    (prose-aware) view; code files are scanned in full.\n    \"\"\"\n    errs: list[str] = []\n    if not os.path.isdir(agent_dir):\n        return errs\n    agent_file_abs = os.path.abspath(agent_file)\n    for dirpath, dirs, files in os.walk(agent_dir):\n        # Exempt dirs (references/templates/examples) are excluded from agent-\n        # *definition* discovery, but bundled resources are exactly what the\n        # security sweep must cover — descend into hidden dirs (credential\n        # locations such as `.ssh/`/`.aws/`), skipping only VCS/cache trees.\n        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SECURITY_SKIP_DIRS]\n        for fn in files:\n            p = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)\n            if os.path.abspath(p) == agent_file_abs or not is_scannable_text(fn):\n                continue\n            try:\n                with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n                    text = f.read()\n            except OSError:\n                continue\n            is_markdown = fn.lower().endswith(\".md\")\n            for m in find_dangerous_pipes(text, fenced_only=is_markdown):\n                errs.append(\n                    f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Dangerous remote-execution pipe detected in \"\n                    f\"{os.path.relpath(p, agent_dir)} ({m.group(0)[:60]!r}); remove it or annotate \"\n                    \"its block/line with a `<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n                )\n            for m in find_inline_secrets(text):\n                errs.append(\n                    f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Possible inline secret/credential detected in \"\n                    f\"{os.path.relpath(p, agent_dir)} ({m.group(0)[:6]}…); remove it or annotate \"\n                    \"its line with a `<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n                )\n    return errs\n\n\ndef collect_validation_results(agents_dir: str, strict_mode: bool = False) -> dict:\n    agents_dir = os.path.abspath(agents_dir)\n    errors = []\n    if not os.path.isdir(agents_dir):\n        # A wrong/typo'd --dir must FAIL loudly. os.walk on a missing path yields\n        # nothing, so without this guard we'd print \"Checked 0 agents\" and exit 0.\n        errors.append(f\"❌ Scan directory does not exist: {agents_dir}\")\n        return {\n            \"agent_count\": 0,\n            \"warnings\": [],\n            \"advisories\": [],\n            \"errors\": errors,\n            \"strict_mode\": strict_mode,\n        }\n    warnings = []\n    advisories = []\n    agent_count = 0\n\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(agents_dir):\n        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if not d.startswith(\".\") and not is_exempt_dir(os.path.join(root, d))]\n        # Agent definition files: AGENT.md inside a dir, or a top-level *.md\n        if \"AGENT.md\" in files:\n            candidates = [(root, \"AGENT.md\")]\n        else:\n            mds = sorted(f for f in files if f.endswith(\".md\") and f.lower() not in NON_AGENT_DOCS)\n            candidates = [(root, f) for f in mds]\n        for base, fname in candidates:\n            agent_path = os.path.join(base, fname)\n            rel_path = os.path.relpath(agent_path, agents_dir)\n            try:\n                with open(agent_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n                    content = f.read()\n            except Exception as e:\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Unreadable file - {str(e)}\")\n                continue\n\n            # Only AGENT.md is a mandatory marker. A plain *.md is an agent\n            # definition only when it actually carries frontmatter, so library\n            # docs (evolution records, subagent prompt files) are not mistaken\n            # for agents and blamed for \"missing frontmatter\".\n            if fname != \"AGENT.md\" and not re.match(r\"^---\\s*\\n\", content):\n                continue\n\n            agent_count += 1\n            metadata, fm_errors = parse_frontmatter(content)\n            if metadata is None:\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing or malformed YAML frontmatter\")\n                continue\n            for fe in fm_errors:\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: YAML Structure Error - {fe}\")\n\n            dir_name = agent_name_from_path(root) if fname == \"AGENT.md\" else agent_name_from_path(fname)\n            if \"name\" not in metadata:\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing 'name' in frontmatter\")\n            else:\n                n = metadata[\"name\"]\n                if not isinstance(n, str):\n                    errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'name' must be a string, got {type(n).__name__}\")\n                else:\n                    if not VALID_NAME.match(n):\n                        errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'name' must be kebab-case, got '{n}'\")\n                    if n != dir_name:\n                        warnings.append(f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: name '{n}' differs from dir/file name '{dir_name}'\")\n\n            if \"description\" not in metadata or metadata[\"description\"] is None:\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Missing 'description' in frontmatter\")\n            else:\n                desc = metadata[\"description\"]\n                if not isinstance(desc, str):\n                    errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'description' must be a string, got {type(desc).__name__}\")\n                elif len(desc) > 300:\n                    errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Description is oversized ({len(desc)} chars). Must be concise.\")\n                else:\n                    # Trigger-surface discipline:\n                    # description is the only always-loaded field — keep it single-line\n                    # and free of angle-bracket placeholders so trigger matching stays\n                    # reliable. Advisory only, never a failure.\n                    if any(ch in desc for ch in (\"<\", \">\")):\n                        advisories.append(\n                            f\"ℹ️  {rel_path}: Description contains '<'/'>' placeholder chars — \"\n                            \"these distort trigger matching (use plain wording, not placeholders).\"\n                        )\n                    if \"\\n\" in desc:\n                        advisories.append(\n                            f\"ℹ️  {rel_path}: Description is multi-line — keep it single-line \"\n                            \"(description is the only unconditionally loaded trigger surface).\"\n                        )\n\n            if \"color\" in metadata:\n                color = metadata[\"color\"]\n                if not isinstance(color, str) or not (VALID_COLOR.match(color) or color in COLOR_THEMES):\n                    themes = \"/\".join(COLOR_THEMES)\n                    errors.append(\n                        f\"❌ {rel_path}: 'color' must be a hex color (#RRGGBB, quoted as \"\n                        f'\"#DC2626\") or one of {themes}, got {color!r}'\n                    )\n\n            risk = metadata.get(\"risk\")\n            if risk == \"offensive\" and not any(p.search(content) for p in SECURITY_DISCLAIMER_PATTERNS):\n                errors.append(f\"🚨 {rel_path}: OFFENSIVE AGENT MISSING THE AUTHORIZED-USE DISCLAIMER\")\n\n            # version/date_added are NOT carried in AGENT.md frontmatter: lifecycle\n            # accounting is git-based, and provenance lives in the library's\n            # creation-record ledger (agents/AGENTS-RECORDS.md). Unknown leftover\n            # fields are simply ignored (forward-compatible).\n\n            body = content.split(\"---\", 2)[2] if content.startswith(\"---\") else content\n            if not body.strip():\n                errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Agent body is empty (identity/boundary sections required)\")\n\n            if not any(p.search(content) for p in BOUNDARY_PATTERNS):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: Missing '## 职责范围' section (identity boundary required)\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n            elif not (any(p.search(content) for p in MUST_DO_PATTERNS) and any(p.search(content) for p in REFUSE_PATTERNS)):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: '职责范围' should declare both 必须做 and 拒绝做\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n\n            if not any(p.search(content) for p in PERMISSION_PATTERNS) and not any(\n                p.search(content) for p in TOOLS_FIELD_PATTERNS\n            ):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: No tools/permission declaration (least-privilege principle)\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n\n            perm_map = metadata.get(\"permission\")\n            if isinstance(perm_map, dict) and \"*\" not in perm_map:\n                # Full-matrix discipline (advisory, never a failure): prefer the\n                # `\"*\": deny` default-reject + explicit per-key allow/deny shape\n                # (UEGameStudio security-engineer example) over a sparse map.\n                advisories.append(\n                    f\"ℹ️  {rel_path}: permission 缺默认拒绝 `\\\"*\\\": deny`——建议用全量矩阵 \"\n                    \"(默认拒绝 + 逐键显式 allow/deny，见 references/agent-template.md)\"\n                )\n\n            if not any(p.search(content) for p in COLLAB_PATTERNS):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: Missing '## 协作协议' section (when called / how to report)\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n            elif not any(p.search(content) for p in ESCALATION_PATTERNS):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: '协作协议' should declare an escalation path (when to hand back to human)\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n\n            if not any(p.search(content) for p in COMPLETION_PATTERNS):\n                msg = f\"⚠️  {rel_path}: Missing '## 完成标准' section (verifiable acceptance criteria)\"\n                (errors if strict_mode else warnings).append(msg.replace(\"⚠️\", \"❌\") if strict_mode else msg)\n\n            # Links and backtick refs shown inside a fenced code block are\n            # illustrative (a doc teaching syntax) and exempt from on-disk checks.\n            fenced_spans = fenced_ranges(content, closed_only=True)\n\n            def _is_in_fence(pos: int) -> bool:\n                return any(s <= pos < e for s, e in fenced_spans)\n\n            for m in re.finditer(r\"\\[[^\\]]*\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)\", content):\n                if _is_in_fence(m.start()):\n                    continue\n                link = m.group(1).strip()\n                # CommonMark allows an optional title after the destination\n                # (`[x](path \"Title\")`). Strip it so a valid titled link is not\n                # mistaken for a path containing spaces and reported dangling.\n                link = re.sub(r\"\\s+(?:\\\"[^\\\"]*\\\"|'[^']*'|\\([^)]*\\))\\s*$\", \"\", link).strip()\n                link_clean = link.split(\"#\")[0].strip()\n                if not link_clean or link_clean.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\", \"mailto:\", \"<\", \">\")):\n                    continue\n                if os.path.isabs(link_clean):\n                    continue\n                target_path = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root, link_clean))\n                if not os.path.exists(target_path):\n                    errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Dangling link detected. Path '{link_clean}' does not exist locally.\")\n\n            backtick_refs = set()\n            for m in BACKTICK_REF_RE.finditer(content):\n                ref = m.group(1)\n                if ref.startswith(\"http\") or \"/\" not in ref or _is_in_fence(m.start()):\n                    continue\n                backtick_refs.add(ref)\n            for ref in sorted(backtick_refs):\n                ref_clean = ref.split(\"#\")[0].strip()\n                if (\n                    not ref_clean\n                    or ref_clean.startswith(\"~/\")\n                    or any(ch in ref_clean for ch in (\"<\", \">\", \"*\", \"?\", \"…\"))\n                    or \"xxx\" in ref_clean\n                    or \"your-agent-name\" in ref_clean\n                ):\n                    continue\n                if os.path.isabs(ref_clean):\n                    continue\n                # Resolve relative to the agent's own directory only. Falling back\n                # to this validator's own SKILL_ROOT would let an agent \"borrow\" a\n                # path that only exists inside agent-creator — a false pass that\n                # hides dangling references.\n                target = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root, ref_clean))\n                if not os.path.exists(target):\n                    errors.append(f\"❌ {rel_path}: Backtick reference '{ref_clean}' does not exist locally.\")\n\n            # Security scan: inline secrets everywhere; dangerous remote-exec\n            # pipes in code context. Local `<!-- security-allowlist -->` markers\n            # excuse only the annotated line/block.\n            for m in find_dangerous_pipes(content):\n                errors.append(\n                    f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Dangerous remote-execution pipe detected \"\n                    f\"({m.group(0)[:60]!r}); remove it or annotate its block/line with a \"\n                    \"`<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n                )\n            for m in find_inline_secrets(content):\n                errors.append(\n                    f\"🚨 {rel_path}: Possible inline secret/credential detected \"\n                    f\"({m.group(0)[:6]}…); remove it or annotate its line with a \"\n                    \"`<!-- security-allowlist -->` note.\"\n                )\n            if fname == \"AGENT.md\":\n                errors.extend(check_dir_security(base, agent_path, rel_path))\n\n    if agent_count == 0:\n        # Scanning zero agent definitions is almost always a wrong target (typo'd\n        # --dir, or run from a directory that holds no AGENT.md). Fail loudly\n        # instead of a green, empty release gate. This holds whether the target\n        # came from --dir or from the default (current working directory).\n        #\n        # A skill-form product root (SKILL.md present, e.g. agent-creator itself\n        # installed into a client's skills/ dir) legitimately holds no AGENT.md —\n        # This validator only applies to agent LIBRARIES. Pointing it there is a\n        # usage error, so still fail, but say so explicitly instead of letting the\n        # user suspect the source tree is incomplete.\n        hint = \"\"\n        if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(agents_dir, \"SKILL.md\")):\n            hint = (\n                \" The target is a skill-form product root (SKILL.md present, no \"\n                \"AGENT.md), not an agent library: agent-creator is a skill installed \"\n                \"into the client's skills/ dir, and agent_validate.py only validates \"\n                \"agent definitions. To self-check an installed agent-creator, run the \"\n                \"roundtrip from SKILL.md 自安装 step 4 (agent_create.py → \"\n                \"agent_validate.py --dir <生成的 install-check 目录>), or point --dir \"\n                \"at an agents/ library.\"\n            )\n        errors.append(\n            f\"❌ No agent definitions found under: {agents_dir} \"\n            \"(wrong --dir? point it at a directory containing AGENT.md; the default \"\n            \"target is the current working directory)\"\n            f\"{hint}\"\n        )\n\n    return {\n        \"agent_count\": agent_count,\n        \"warnings\": warnings,\n        \"advisories\": advisories,\n        \"errors\": errors,\n        \"strict_mode\": strict_mode,\n    }\n\n\ndef validate_agents(agents_dir: str, strict_mode: bool = False) -> bool:\n    configure_utf8_output()\n    print(f\"🔍 Validating agents in: {agents_dir}\")\n    print(f\"⚙️  Mode: {'STRICT (CI)' if strict_mode else 'Standard (Dev)'}\")\n\n    results = collect_validation_results(agents_dir, strict_mode=strict_mode)\n    warnings = results[\"warnings\"]\n    advisories = results[\"advisories\"]\n    errors = results[\"errors\"]\n    agent_count = results[\"agent_count\"]\n\n    print(f\"\\n📊 Checked {agent_count} agents.\")\n\n    if warnings:\n        print(f\"\\n⚠️  Found {len(warnings)} Warnings:\")\n        for w in warnings:\n            print(w)\n    if advisories:\n        print(f\"\\nℹ️  Found {len(advisories)} Advisories:\")\n        for advisory in advisories:\n            print(advisory)\n    if errors:\n        print(f\"\\n❌ Found {len(errors)} Critical Errors:\")\n        for e in errors:\n            print(e)\n        return False\n    if strict_mode and warnings:\n        print(\"\\n❌ STRICT MODE: Failed due to warnings.\")\n        return False\n\n    print(\"\\n✨ All agents passed validation!\")\n    return True\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Validate agent definitions (AGENT.md)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--strict\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Fail on warnings (for CI)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dir\", default=None,\n                        help=\"Agents directory to validate (default: the current working directory)\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    # Default to CWD so running from an agents library root (where AGENT.md files\n    # live) works out of the box; scanning 0 definitions fails loudly regardless.\n    agents_dir = args.dir or os.getcwd()\n\n    success = validate_agents(agents_dir, strict_mode=args.strict)\n    if not success:\n        sys.exit(1)","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":512,"totalLines":512,"truncated":false}},"title":"Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_validate.py","time":{"start":1790681734755,"end":1790681734863}},"id":"prt_0ecf2db140016ytLLXwG00chvw","sessionID":"ses_f130e8eecffevMANL5K76zMuzF","messageID":"msg_0ecf27045001sRPANaKnicXXqi"}}

Packaging = adapt the\n4: AGENT.md frontmatter to the client's schema, copy the whole agent tree (minus\n5: caches), and run a per-client post-check so a package that would not load is\n6: never emitted (transform + post-check discipline), reusing the schema\n7: checks/constants of `agent_adapt.py`.\n8: \n9: Frontmatter handling (agent-specific):\n10:   - A `tools` list whose entries are client labels (`claude`/`opencode`/...) is\n11:     \"supported-clients\" metadata, NOT a tool whitelist. It is dropped for\n12:     claude/opencode and left as-is for codex/deepseek (best-effort passthrough).\n13:   - A `tools` list of actual tool names IS a least-privilege whitelist:\n14:       * opencode: the whitelist is merged into a per-tool `permission` map\n15:         (whitelisted tool -> allow, other tool-class keys -> deny; an explicit\n16:         `permission` entry always wins). A bare `permission` **string shorthand**\n17:         is NOT kept as a global rule (that would widen/loosen) — the whitelist is\n18:         materialized into per-tool rules instead. This fixes the privilege\n19:         widening in `agent_adapt.py`, where a string shorthand kept the whitelist\n20:         from being merged at all.\n21:       * claude: the whitelist becomes a comma-separated Claude tool-name string;\n22:         a provider-prefixed `model` is reduced to a claude alias (sonnet/opus/\n23:         haiku/inherit) or dropped when unmappable.\n24:   - codex/deepseek: no official agent frontmatter convention — YAML sanity check\n25:     plus a generic name/description check, body passes through.\n26: \n27: Usage:\n28:     python scripts/agent_package.py <agent_dir | AGENT.md> \\\n29:         --client claude [--client opencode ...] --out <dir> [--zip]\n30: \n31: Output layout: <out>/<client>/<agent-name>/  (plus <out>/<client>/<agent-name>.zip\n32: with --zip). <agent-name> = frontmatter `name` when it is valid kebab-case, else\n33: the input directory/file stem. Exit code 0 = all requested clients packaged;\n34: 1 = a package failed (nothing partial for that client); 2 = bad arguments.\n35: \"\"\"\n36: \n37: import argparse\n38: import io\n39: import re\n40: import shutil\n41: import sys\n42: import zipfile\n43: from pathlib import Path\n44: \n45: import yaml\n46: \n47: _SCRIPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n48: if str(_SCRIPTS_DIR) not in sys.path:\n49:     sys.path.insert(0, str(_SCRIPTS_DIR))\n50: \n51: import agent_adapt as _adapt  # noqa: E402  (shared constants + schema post-checks)\n52: \n53: FRONTMATTER_RE = re.compile(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)\", re.DOTALL)\n54: NAME_RE = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\")\n55: \n56: ALL_CLIENTS = _adapt.ALL_CLIENTS\n57: TRANSFORM_CLIENTS = _adapt.TRANSFORM_CLIENTS\n58: # Client labels that may appear in an agent's `tools:` field meaning \"supported\n59: # clients\" — never a tool whitelist.\n60: CLIENT_LABELS = set(ALL_CLIENTS)\n61: \n62: # Copied tree noise that must never be shipped.\n63: _IGNORE_NAMES = {\"__pycache__\", \".git\", \".pytest_cache\", \".mypy_cache\", \".ruff_cache\"}\n64: \n65: \n66: class PackageError(ValueError):\n67:     \"\"\"Raised when an agent cannot be packaged for a target client.\"\"\"\n68: \n69: \n70: def configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n71:     _adapt.configure_utf8_output()\n72: \n73: \n74: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n75: # Frontmatter\n76: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n77: \n78: def split_frontmatter(content: str):\n79:     \"\"\"Return (fm_text or None, body). fm_text None = no well-formed block.\"\"\"\n80:     m = FRONTMATTER_RE.match(content)\n81:     if not m:\n82:         return None, content\n83:     return m.group(1), content[m.end():]\n84: \n85: \n86: def parse_frontmatter(fm_text: str, origin: str) -> dict:\n87:     try:\n88:         data = yaml.safe_load(fm_text)\n89:     except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n90:         raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: invalid YAML frontmatter: {e}\") from e\n91:     if data is None:\n92:         data = {}\n93:     if not isinstance(data, dict):\n94:         raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: frontmatter must be a YAML mapping, got {type(data).__name__}\")\n95:     return data\n96: \n97: \n98: def normalize_tools(value, origin: str):\n99:     \"\"\"Canonicalize `tools` to a lowercase name list; a bool map passes through.\"\"\"\n100:     if isinstance(value, dict):\n101:         return value\n102:     if isinstance(value, str):\n103:         return [p.strip().lower() for p in value.split(\",\") if metadata.\"\"\"\n116:     return bool(tools) and all(isinstance(t, str) and t.lower() in CLIENT_LABELS for t in tools)\n117: \n118: \n119: def _translate_schema_check(fn, fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n120:     try:\n121:         fn(fm, origin)\n122:     except _adapt.AgentFormatError as e:\n123:         raise PackageError(str(e)) from e\n124: \n125: \n126: # isinstance(name, str) or not NAME_RE.match(name):\n133:         raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: agent requires a kebab-case 'name' in frontmatter, got {name!r}\")\n134:     desc = def check_opencode_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n140:     _check_common(fm, origin)\n141:     _translate_schema_check(_adapt.check_opencode_frontmatter, fm, origin)\n142: \n143: \n144: def check_claude_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n145:     _check_common(fm, origin)\n146:     _translate_schema_check(_adapt.check_claude_frontmatter, fm, origin)\n147: \n148: \n149: def check_generic_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n150:     _check_common(fm, origin)\n151: \n152: \n153: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n154: # Transforms\n155: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n156: \n157: def _merge_whitelist_into_permission(perm, tools: list[str]) -> dict:\n158:     \"\"\"Materialize a tool whitelist as per-tool permission rules.\n159: \n160:     Whitelisted tool-class keys -> allow; every other tool-class key -> deny; an\n161:     explicit entry in an existing Packaging = adapt the\nAGENT.md frontmatter to the client's schema, copy the whole agent tree (minus\ncaches), and run a per-client post-check so a package that would not load is\nnever emitted (transform + post-check discipline), reusing the schema\nchecks/constants of `agent_adapt.py`.\n\nFrontmatter handling (agent-specific):\n  - A `tools` list whose entries are client labels (`claude`/`opencode`/...) is\n    \"supported-clients\" metadata, NOT a tool whitelist. It is dropped for\n    claude/opencode and left as-is for codex/deepseek (best-effort passthrough).\n  - A `tools` list of actual tool names IS a least-privilege whitelist:\n      * opencode: the whitelist is merged into a per-tool `permission` map\n        (whitelisted tool -> allow, other tool-class keys -> deny; an explicit\n        `permission` entry always wins). A bare `permission` **string shorthand**\n        is NOT kept as a global rule (that would widen/loosen) — the whitelist is\n        materialized into per-tool rules instead. This fixes the privilege\n        widening in `agent_adapt.py`, where a string shorthand kept the whitelist\n        from being merged at all.\n      * claude: the whitelist becomes a comma-separated Claude tool-name string;\n        a provider-prefixed `model` is reduced to a claude alias (sonnet/opus/\n        haiku/inherit) or dropped when unmappable.\n  - codex/deepseek: no official agent frontmatter convention — YAML sanity check\n    plus a generic name/description check, body passes through.\n\nUsage:\n    python scripts/agent_package.py <agent_dir | AGENT.md> \\\n        --client claude [--client opencode ...] --out <dir> [--zip]\n\nOutput layout: <out>/<client>/<agent-name>/  (plus <out>/<client>/<agent-name>.zip\nwith --zip). <agent-name> = frontmatter `name` when it is valid kebab-case, else\nthe input directory/file stem. Exit code 0 = all requested clients packaged;\n1 = a package failed (nothing partial for that client); 2 = bad arguments.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport io\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport sys\nimport zipfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport yaml\n\n_SCRIPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nif str(_SCRIPTS_DIR) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_SCRIPTS_DIR))\n\nimport agent_adapt as _adapt  # noqa: E402  (shared constants + schema post-checks)\n\nFRONTMATTER_RE = re.compile(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)\", re.DOTALL)\nNAME_RE = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\")\n\nALL_CLIENTS = _adapt.ALL_CLIENTS\nTRANSFORM_CLIENTS = _adapt.TRANSFORM_CLIENTS\n# Client labels that may appear in an agent's `tools:` field meaning \"supported\n# clients\" — never a tool whitelist.\nCLIENT_LABELS = set(ALL_CLIENTS)\n\n# Copied tree noise that must never be shipped.\n_IGNORE_NAMES = {\"__pycache__\", \".git\", \".pytest_cache\", \".mypy_cache\", \".ruff_cache\"}\n\n\nclass PackageError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when an agent cannot be packaged for a target client.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n    _adapt.configure_utf8_output()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Frontmatter\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef split_frontmatter(content: str):\n    \"\"\"Return (fm_text or None, body). fm_text None = no well-formed block.\"\"\"\n    m = FRONTMATTER_RE.match(content)\n    if not m:\n        return None, content\n    return m.group(1), content[m.end():]\n\n\ndef parse_frontmatter(fm_text: str, origin: str) -> dict:\n    try:\n        data = yaml.safe_load(fm_text)\n    except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n        raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: invalid YAML frontmatter: {e}\") from e\n    if data is None:\n        data = {}\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: frontmatter must be a YAML mapping, got {type(data).__name__}\")\n    return data\n\n\ndef normalize_tools(value, origin: str):\n    \"\"\"Canonicalize `tools` to a lowercase name list; a bool map passes through.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(value, dict):\n        return value\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        return [p.strip().lower() for p in value.split(\",\") if p.strip()]\n    if isinstance(value, list):\n        names = []\n        for item in value:\n            if not isinstance(item, str):\n                raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: 'tools' entries must be tool names, got {item!r}\")\n            names.append(item.strip().lower())\n        return names\n    raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: unsupported 'tools' form: {type(value).__name__}\")\n\n\ndef _is_client_label_list(tools) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when a tools list is really the repo's supported-clients metadata.\"\"\"\n    return bool(tools) and all(isinstance(t, str) and t.lower() in CLIENT_LABELS for t in tools)\n\n\ndef _translate_schema_check(fn, fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        fn(fm, origin)\n    except _adapt.AgentFormatError as e:\n        raise PackageError(str(e)) from e\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-client post-checks (never emit a package the client would reject)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _check_common(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n    name = fm.get(\"name\")\n    if not isinstance(name, str) or not NAME_RE.match(name):\n        raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: agent requires a kebab-case 'name' in frontmatter, got {name!r}\")\n    desc = fm.get(\"description\")\n    if not isinstance(desc, str) or not desc.strip():\n        raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: agent requires a non-empty 'description' string\")\n\n\ndef check_opencode_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n    _check_common(fm, origin)\n    _translate_schema_check(_adapt.check_opencode_frontmatter, fm, origin)\n\n\ndef check_claude_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n    _check_common(fm, origin)\n    _translate_schema_check(_adapt.check_claude_frontmatter, fm, origin)\n\n\ndef check_generic_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n    _check_common(fm, origin)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Transforms\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _merge_whitelist_into_permission(perm, tools: list[str]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Materialize a tool whitelist as per-tool permission rules.\n\n    Whitelisted tool-class keys -> allow; every other tool-class key -> deny; an\n    explicit entry in an existing `permission` map always wins. A bare permission\n    string is intentionally NOT honored as a global rule: keeping e.g. `allow`\n    globally would grant un-whitelisted tools and is exactly the privilege\n    widening this merge prevents.\n    \"\"\"\n    merged = dict(perm) if isinstance(perm, dict) else {}\n    allowed = set()\n    extra = []\n    for tool in tools:\n        key = _adapt.OPENCODE_TOOL_ALIASES.get(tool, tool)\n        if key in _adapt.OPENCODE_TOOL_KEYS:\n            allowed.add(key)\n        else:\n            extra.append(key)\n    for key in _adapt.OPENCODE_TOOL_KEYS:\n        if key not in merged:\n            merged[key] = \"allow\" if key in allowed else \"deny\"\n    for key in extra:\n        merged.setdefault(key, \"allow\")\n    return merged\n\n\ndef adapt_for_opencode(fm: dict, notes: list, origin: str) -> None:\n    if \"tools\" in fm:\n        tools = normalize_tools(fm.pop(\"tools\"), origin)\n        if isinstance(tools, dict):\n            fm[\"tools\"] = tools\n            notes.append(\"opencode: kept legacy tools bool-map (deprecated; prefer permission)\")\n        elif _is_client_label_list(tools):\n            notes.append(\n                \"opencode: 'tools' listed supported clients (metadata); dropped for this client\"\n            )\n        else:\n            perm = fm.get(\"permission\")\n            if isinstance(perm, str):\n                notes.append(\n                    f\"opencode: dropped global permission shorthand {perm!r} (would widen); \"\n                    \"tools whitelist merged into per-tool permission\"\n                )\n            merged = _merge_whitelist_into_permission(perm, tools)\n            fm[\"permission\"] = merged\n            denied = [k for k in _adapt.OPENCODE_TOOL_KEYS if merged.get(k) == \"deny\"]\n            notes.append(\n                \"opencode: tools whitelist -> permission ({allow} allow{denied})\".format(\n                    allow=\", \".join(\n                        sorted(t for t, v in merged.items() if v == \"allow\" and t in _adapt.OPENCODE_TOOL_KEYS)\n                    ) or \"-\",\n                    denied=f\"; deny {', '.join(denied)}\" if denied else \"\",\n                )\n            )\n    check_opencode_frontmatter(fm, origin)\n\n\ndef adapt_for_claude(fm: dict, notes: list, origin: str) -> None:\n    if \"tools\" in fm:\n        tools = normalize_tools(fm[\"tools\"], origin)\n        if isinstance(tools, dict):\n            names = [str(k).strip().lower() for k, v in tools.items() if v is True]\n        elif _is_client_label_list(tools):\n            names = None\n            fm.pop(\"tools\")\n            notes.append(\"claude: 'tools' listed supported clients (metadata); dropped for this client\")\n        else:\n            names = tools\n        if names is not None:\n            mapped, dropped = [], []\n            for tool in names:\n                claude_name = _adapt.CLAUDE_TOOL_NAMES.get(tool)\n                if claude_name:\n                    if claude_name not in mapped:\n                        mapped.append(claude_name)\n                else:\n                    dropped.append(tool)\n            if not mapped:\n                raise PackageError(\n                    f\"{origin}: claude 'tools' whitelist {names} maps to no Claude tool names; \"\n                    \"refusing to omit 'tools' (absent tools = ALL tools in Claude)\"\n                )\n            fm[\"tools\"] = \", \".join(mapped)\n            note = f\"claude: tools whitelist -> \\\"{fm['tools']}\\\"\"\n            if dropped:\n                note += f\" (no Claude equivalent, dropped: {', '.join(dropped)})\"\n            notes.append(note)\n    if \"model\" in fm:\n        model = str(fm[\"model\"]).strip().lower()\n        if model in _adapt.CLAUDE_MODEL_ALIASES:\n            fm[\"model\"] = model\n        else:\n            alias = next((a for a in (\"sonnet\", \"opus\", \"haiku\") if a in model), None)\n            if alias:\n                notes.append(f\"claude: model {fm['model']!r} -> '{alias}'\")\n                fm[\"model\"] = alias\n            else:\n                notes.append(f\"claude: unmappable model {fm['model']!r} dropped (inherits default)\")\n                del fm[\"model\"]\n    check_claude_frontmatter(fm, origin)\n\n\ndef adapt_agent_markdown(content: str, client: str, origin: str = \"AGENT.md\"):\n    \"\"\"Return (adapted_content, notes) for one target client.\n\n    Raises PackageError on malformed frontmatter or a result the client schema\n    would reject. codex/deepseek get a YAML + name/description sanity check and a\n    best-effort passthrough.\n    \"\"\"\n    client = (client or \"\").lower()\n    if client not in ALL_CLIENTS:\n        raise PackageError(f\"unknown client: {client!r} (choose from {', '.join(ALL_CLIENTS)})\")\n    fm_text, body = split_frontmatter(content)\n    if fm_text is None:\n        raise PackageError(f\"{origin}: no frontmatter block found (an agent requires name/description)\")\n    fm = parse_frontmatter(fm_text, origin)\n    notes: list = []\n    if client == \"opencode\":\n        adapt_for_opencode(fm, notes, origin)\n    elif client == \"claude\":\n        adapt_for_claude(fm, notes, origin)\n    else:\n        check_generic_frontmatter(fm, origin)\n        notes.append(f\"{client}: no official agent frontmatter schema (best-effort); kept after YAML check\")\n    dumped = yaml.safe_dump(fm, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False, width=4096)\n    return f\"---\\n{dumped}---\\n{body}\", notes\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Filesystem packaging\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _ignore(_dirpath, names):\n    return [n for n in names if n in _IGNORE_NAMES or n.endswith((\".pyc\", \".pyo\"))]\n\n\ndef _copy_tree(src: Path, dst: Path) -> None:\n    shutil.copytree(src, dst, ignore=_ignore, dirs_exist_ok=True)\n\n\ndef _make_zip(src_dir: Path, zip_path: Path) -> None:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:\n        for p in sorted(src_dir.rglob(\"*\")):\n            if p.is_file():\n                z.write(p, arcname=str(Path(src_dir.name) / p.relative_to(src_dir)))\n\n\ndef _resolve_agent(agent_path: Path):\n    \"\"\"Return (agent_file, base_dir, is_dir) for a dir or a bare AGENT.md file.\"\"\"\n    if agent_path.is_dir():\n        agent_file = agent_path / \"AGENT.md\"\n        if not agent_file.is_file():\n            raise PackageError(f\"no AGENT.md found in: {agent_path}\")\n        return agent_file, agent_path, True\n    if agent_path.is_file():\n        return agent_path, agent_path.parent, False\n    raise PackageError(f\"agent path does not exist: {agent_path}\")\n\n\ndef package_agent(agent_path, clients, out_dir, make_zip: bool = False) -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Package one agent dir (or bare AGENT.md) for each client; return written dirs.\"\"\"\n    agent_path = Path(agent_path)\n    agent_file, base_dir, is_dir = _resolve_agent(agent_path)\n    content = agent_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8-sig\")\n    origin = agent_file.name if not is_dir else f\"{base_dir.name}/{agent_file.name}\"\n\n    out_dir = Path(out_dir)\n    if out_dir.exists() and not out_dir.is_dir():\n        raise PackageError(f\"--out is an existing file, expected a directory: {out_dir}\")\n    out_resolved = out_dir.resolve()\n    base_resolved = base_dir.resolve()\n    if out_resolved == base_resolved or base_resolved in out_resolved.parents:\n        raise PackageError(f\"--out must not be inside the agent directory: {out_dir}\")\n\n    fm_text, _body = split_frontmatter(content)\n    fm = parse_frontmatter(fm_text, origin) if fm_text is not None else {}\n    raw_name = fm.get(\"name\")\n    if isinstance(raw_name, str) and NAME_RE.match(raw_name):\n        agent_name = raw_name\n    else:\n        agent_name = agent_path.name if is_dir else agent_path.stem\n\n    written: list[Path] = []\n    for client in clients:\n        adapted, notes = adapt_agent_markdown(content, client, origin=origin)\n        target = out_dir / client / agent_name\n        if target.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(target)\n        if is_dir:\n            _copy_tree(base_dir, target)\n        else:\n            target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        (target / \"AGENT.md\").write_text(adapted, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        for note in notes:\n            print(f\"ℹ️  [{client}] {note}\")\n        if make_zip:\n            zip_path = target.with_suffix(\".zip\")\n            _make_zip(target, zip_path)\n            print(f\"📦 {zip_path}\")\n        print(f\"✅ [{client}] wrote {target}\")\n        written.append(target)\n    return written\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    configure_utf8_output()\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Package an agent directory (or bare AGENT.md) for one or more LLM \"\n                    \"clients (adapts AGENT.md frontmatter + copies the tree + post-checks).\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"agent_path\", help=\"Agent directory containing AGENT.md, or a bare AGENT.md file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--client\", action=\"append\", required=True,\n                        help=f\"Target client (repeatable / comma-separated): {', '.join(ALL_CLIENTS)}\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--out\", required=True, help=\"Output directory (per-client subdirs are created)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--zip\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Also write a .zip per client package\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    clients = []\n    for raw in args.client:\n        for part in raw.split(\",\"):\n            part = part.strip().lower()\n            if part:\n                clients.append(part)\n    unknown = [c for c in clients if c not in ALL_CLIENTS]\n    if unknown:\n        print(f\"❌ unknown client(s): {', '.join(unknown)} (choose from {', '.join(ALL_CLIENTS)})\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n    if not clients:\n        print(\"❌ no client specified\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n\n    try:\n        package_agent(args.agent_path, clients, args.out, make_zip=args.zip)\n    except PackageError as e:\n        print(f\"❌ {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 1\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":397,"totalLines":397,"truncated":false}},"title":"Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_package.py","time":{"start":1790681734807,"end":1790681734888}},"id":"prt_0ecf2de66001oGxawqN40kkwd1","sessionID":"ses_f130e8eecffevMANL5K76zMuzF","messageID":"msg_0ecf27045001sRPANaKnicXXqi"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1790681760317,"sessionID":"ses_f130e8eecffevMANL5K76zMuzF","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_00_U6XSZtC9NCzXkNRdGbvN9442","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"C:\\Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_adapt.py"},"output":"<path>C:\\Users\\Administrator\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\agent-live-env-ml91hn1x\\temp\\scenario-916hc38g\\.opencode\\skills\\agent-creator\\scripts\\agent_adapt.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"Per-client frontmatter adaptation for agent installs (agent-creator stage 7).\n2: \n3: The repo canonical AGENT.md keeps a tool whitelist (`tools: [read, grep, ...]`)\n4: and a provider-prefixed model, but clients disagree on the frontmatter schema\n5: and placing the canonical form verbatim can make the agent unloadable:\n6: \n7:   - opencode: AgentConfig requires `tools` as object<str, bool> (deprecated in\n8:     favor of `permission`). A YAML list under `tools:` fails strict validation\n9:     and the agent does not load. Transform: drop the whitelist and materialize\n10:     it into `permission` — whitelisted tool keys become `allow`, remaining\n11:     tool-like permission keys become `deny`; explicit permission entries win.\n12:   - claude: `tools` must be a comma-separated string of Claude tool names\n13:     (Read, Grep, Bash, ...); `model` must be an alias (sonnet/opus/haiku/\n14:     inherit) or absent.\n15:   - codex / deepseek: no official agent frontmatter convention (best-effort) —\n16:     YAML sanity check only, body passes through unchanged (a leading UTF-8 BOM is\n17:     dropped on read, and text-mode newline translation applies).\n18: \n19: Every transform ends with a per-client post-check derived from the client's\n20: published schema; violations exit 1 (fail loudly, never emit a file that would\n21: not load).\n22: \n23: Adapted from tools/scripts/agent_format.py (external personal-workflow repo).\n24: (the frontmatter adapter of that repo's install/update launchers). This repo\n25: installs agents by copying into the client agents dir (no install launcher),\n26: so this script is the conversion step you run BEFORE the copy.\n27: \n28: Usage:\n29:     python scripts/agent_adapt.py <agent.md 或 代理目录> --client <claude|opencode|codex|deepseek> [--out <落点文件>]\n30: \n31: Exit code 0 = adapted (or verbatim passthrough for codex/deepseek). 1 = the\n32: file could not be made valid for the target client (nothing written).\n33: \"\"\"\n34: \n35: import argparse\n36: import io\n37: import os\n38: import re\n39: import sys\n40: \n41: import yaml\n42: \n43: FRONTMATTER_RE = re.compile(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n?---(?:\\s*\\n|$)\", re.DOTALL)\n44: \n45: \n46: class AgentFormatError(ValueError):\n47:     \"\"\"Raised when agent frontmatter cannot be made valid for the target client.\"\"\"\n48: \n49: \n50: # opencode permission keys that gate plain tools. Mechanism keys\n51: # (external_directory, doom_loop, lsp) are never rewritten automatically.\n52: OPENCODE_TOOL_KEYS = (\n53:     \"read\",\n54:     \"edit\",\n55:     \"glob\",\n56:     \"grep\",\n57:     \"list\",\n58:     \"bash\",\n59:     \"task\",\n60:     \"webfetch\",\n61:     \"websearch\",\n62:     \"todowrite\",\n63:     \"question\",\n64:     \"skill\",\n65: )\n66: # Repo whitelist tool names that fold into another permission key on opencode.\n67: OPENCODE_TOOL_ALIASES = {\"write\": \"edit\", \"patch\": \"edit\"}\n68: OPENCODE_MODES = (\"primary\", \"subagent\", \"all\")\n69: OPENCODE_THEMES = (\"primary\", \"secondary\", \"accent\", \"success\", \"warning\", \"error\", \"info\")\n70: HEX_COLOR_RE = re.compile(r\"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$\")\n71: PERMISSION_ACTIONS = (\"allow\", \"ask\", \"deny\")\n72: \n73: # canonical lowercase tool name -> Claude tool name.\n74: CLAUDE_TOOL_NAMES = {\n75:     \"read\": \"Read\",\n76:     \"write\": \"Write\",\n77:     \"edit\": \"Edit\",\n78:     \"bash\": \"Bash\",\n79:     \"glob\": \"Glob\",\n80:     \"grep\": \"Grep\",\n81:     \"webfetch\": \"WebFetch\",\n82:     \"websearch\": \"WebSearch\",\n83:     \"task\": \"Task\",\n84:     \"todowrite\": \"TodoWrite\",\n85: }\n86: CLAUDE_MODEL_ALIASES = (\"sonnet\", \"opus\", \"haiku\", \"inherit\")\n87: CLAUDE_NAME_RE = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$\")\n88: \n89: # Clients whose copies are transformed; others pass through verbatim.\n90: TRANSFORM_CLIENTS = (\"claude\", \"opencode\")\n91: ALL_CLIENTS = (\"claude\", \"opencode\", \"codex\", \"deepseek\")\n92: \n93: \n94: def configure_utf8_output() -> None:\n95:     if sys.platform != \"win32\":\n96:         return\n97:     for stream_name in (\"stdout\", \"stderr\"):\n98:         stream = getattr(sys, stream_name)\n99:         try:\n100:             stream.reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\")\n101:             continue\n102:         except Exception:\n103:             pass\n104:         buffer = getattr(stream, \"buffer\", None)\n105:         if buffer is not None:\n106:             setattr(sys, stream_name, io.TextIOWrapper(buffer, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"backslashreplace\"))\n107: \n108: \n109: def split_frontmatter(content: str):\n110:     \"\"\"Return (fm_text or None, body). fm_text None = no well-formed block.\"\"\"\n111:     m = FRONTMATTER_RE.match(content)\n112:     if not m:\n113:         return None, content\n114:     return m.group(1), content[m.end():]\n115: \n116: \n117: def parse_frontmatter(fm_text: str, origin: str) -> dict:\n118:     try:\n119:         data = yaml.safe_load(fm_text)\n120:     except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n121:         raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: invalid YAML frontmatter: {e}\") from e\n122:     if data is None:\n123:         data = {}\n124:     if not isinstance(data, dict):\n125:         raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: frontmatter must be a YAML mapping, got {type(data).__name__}\")\n126:     return data\n127: \n128: \n129: def normalize_tools(value, origin: str):\n130:     \"\"\"Canonicalize `tools` to a lowercase name list; bool maps pass through.\"\"\"\n131:     if isinstance(value, dict):\n132:         return value\n133:     if isinstance(value, str):\n134:         return [p.strip().lower() for p in value.split(\",\") if p.strip()]\n135:     if isinstance(value, list):\n136:         names = []\n137:         for item in value:\n138:             if not isinstance(item, str):\n139:                 raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: 'tools' entries must be tool names, got {item!r}\")\n140:             names.append(item.strip().lower())\n141:         return names\n142:     raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: unsupported 'tools' form: {type(value).__name__}\")\n143: \n144: \n145: def _check_permission(perm, origin: str) -> None:\n146:     if isinstance(perm, str):\n147:         if perm not in PERMISSION_ACTIONS:\n148:             raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: permission must be one of {'/'.join(PERMISSION_ACTIONS)}, got {perm!r}\")\n149:         return\n150:     if isinstance(perm, dict):\n151:         for key, value in perm.items():\n152:             if isinstance(value, str):\n153:                 if value not in PERMISSION_ACTIONS:\n154:                     raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: permission.{key} must be allow/ask/deny, got {value!r}\")\n155:             elif isinstance(value, dict):\n156:                 for pattern, action in value.items():\n157:                     if not isinstance(pattern, str) or not isinstance(action, str) or action not in PERMISSION_ACTIONS:\n158:                         raise AgentFormatError(\n159:                             f\"{origin}: permission.{key} pattern rules must map pattern -> allow/ask/deny\"\n160:                         )\n161:             else:\n162:                 raise AgentFormatError(\n163:                     f\"{origin}: permission.{key} must be a string action or a pattern map, \"\n164:                     f\"got {type(value).__name__}\"\n165:                 )\n166:         return\n167:     raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: permission must be a string action or a map, got {type(perm).__name__}\")\n168: \n169: \n170: def check_opencode_frontmatter(fm: dict, origin: str) -> None:\n171:     \"\"\"Post-check against opencode's published AgentConfig schema.\"\"\"\n172:     if \"tools\" in fm:\n173:         tools = fm[\"tools\"]\n174:         if not isinstance(tools, dict) or any(not isinstance(v, bool) for v in tools.values()):\n175:             raise AgentFormatError(\n176:                 f\"{origin}: opencode 'tools' must be a map of tool-name -> boolean \"\n177:                 \"(arrays/strings are invalid; canonical whitelists are converted automatically)\"\n178:             )\n179:     if \"mode\" in fm and fm[\"mode\"] not in OPENCODE_MODES:\n180:         raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: opencode 'mode' must be one of {'/'.join(OPENCODE_MODES)}, got {fm['mode']!r}\")\n181:     if \"permission\" in fm and fm[\"permission\"] is not None:\n182:         _check_permission(fm[\"permission\"], origin)\n183:     for key in (\"temperature\", \"top_p\"):\n184:         if key in fm and (isinstance(fm[key], bool) or not isinstance(fm[key], (int, float))):\n185:             raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: opencode '{key}' must be a number, got {fm[key]!r}\")\n186:     for key in (\"steps\", \"maxSteps\"):\n187:         if key in fm and (isinstance(fm[key], bool) or not isinstance(fm[key], int) or fm[key] <= 0):\n188:             raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: opencode '{key}' must be a positive integer, got {fm[key]!r}\")\n189:     for key in (\"hidden\", \"disable\"):\n190:         if key in fm and not isinstance(fm[key], bool):\n191:             raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: opencode '{key}' must be a boolean, got {fm[key]!r}\")\n192:     for key in (\"name\", \"description\", \"model\", \"variant\"):\n193:         if key in fm and not isinstance(fm[key], str):\n194:             raise AgentFormatError(f\"{origin}: opencode '{key}' must be a string, got {type(fm[key]).__name__}\")\n195:     if \"color\" in fm:\n196:         color = fm[\"color\"]\n197:         if not isinstance(color, str) or not (HEX_COLOR_RE.match(color) or color in OPENCODE_THEMES):\n198:             raise AgentFormatError(\n199:                 f\"{origin}: opencode 'color' must be a hex color (#RRGGBB) or one of \"\n200:                 f\"{'/'.join(OPENCODE_THEMES)}, got {color!r}\"\n201:             )\n202:     if \"options\" in fm and not
